# Component Inventory Matrix

**Status:** Implementation-ready inventory for the Storybook-only UI library MVP.
**Updated:** 2026-06-06T16:09:52Z.

This file is the master inventory. It intentionally includes components and patterns beyond P0/P1 so future agents can see what belongs in the library, what is landing-only, and what remains deferred. P0/P1 implementation sequencing lives in `36_P0_P1_IMPLEMENTATION_BACKLOG.md`; detailed API contracts live in `35_COMPONENT_API_REFERENCE_MATRIX.md`.

## Priority And Migration Labels

| Label | Meaning |
|---|---|
| P0 | Required for first Storybook review. |
| P1 | Required before any serious app migration plan. |
| P2 | Useful after P0/P1; not a blocker. |
| P3 | Future enhancement or edge case. |
| DEFERRED | Not in the MVP until owner/package/evidence gate is resolved. |
| LANDING_ONLY | Must not leak into app/workshop UI. |
| STORYBOOK_ONLY | Build/review in Storybook only; no production migration yet. |
| FUTURE_ONLY | Keep as future backlog. |
| APP_MIGRATION_DEFERRED | Future route migration needs separate approval. |

## Inventory Gate Notes

- The rows above intentionally overrepresent component coverage so implementation agents do not invent a narrower library by omission.
- P0/P1 rows must be represented in `17_STORYBOOK_STORY_COVERAGE_MATRIX.md` before the Storybook review.
- P2/P3/LANDING_ONLY rows may remain backlog items after the MVP as long as they are not silently migrated into app/workshop surfaces.
- Existing shared primitives with high impact, especially `Button`, `SearchInput`, `Modal`, `ContextMenu`, `Toast`, and `WorkshopTree`, require graph/GitNexus impact and focused tests before source edits.
